function s_getLoadTime() {
	if (!window.s_loadT) {
		var b=new Date().getTime(),o=window.performance?performance.timing:0,a=o?o.requestStart:window.inHeadTS||0;

/* returns object value or false dep. if data layer element exists */
function adbCheckDataLayerElement(p) {
	var s=this,pth=[],root=window.dataLayer,pr;
	try {
		if (!root) return false;
		if (typeof p==='string') pth=p?p.split('.'):[];
		else {
			if (Object.prototype.toString.call(p)==='[object Array]') pth=p;
			else if(p) return false;
		}
		while(pr=pth.shift()) {
			try{
				if (pr in root) root=root[pr];
				else return false;
			} catch(e){_satellite.notify("Error in Page Load Rule 'Global Functions' > 'adbCheckDataLayerElement' :"+e);return false;}
		}
	} catch(e){_satellite.notify("Error in 'Global Functions'>function adbCheckDataLayerElement:"+e)}
	return root;
}
/* replaces special cars with their non-encoded values */
function decodeString(s) {
  if (_checkIfExist(s)) {
  	s=s.replace(/&amp;/g,'&');
  	s=s.replace(/&euro;/g,'€');
  	s=s.replace(/\;/g,':');
  	s=s.replace(/\,/g,'-');
  	s=s.replace(/\s{2,}/g,' ');
  }
  return s;
}
/* appends value to list */
function appendToList(L,v,d,u) {
	m=0;
	d=d?d:',';
	if (!L) L='';
	else {
		if (u) {
    	var i,n,a=s.split(L,d);
    	for (i=0;i<a.length;i++) {
        	n=a[i];
        	m=m||(u==1?(n==v):(n.toLowerCase()==v.toLowerCase()));
    	}
		}
	}
	if (!m) L=L?L+d+v:v;
	return L;
}
/* checks and returns value for element */
function _checkIfExist(el,def,lg) {
	def=def!=''?def:'';
  return (typeof(el)=='undefined'||el==='')?'':el;
}

var j=0;
function _getPRODUCTS(obj) {
	if (_checkIfExist(obj)!='') {
		obj=obj.item;
		if (_checkIfExist(obj)!='') {
			if (obj.length>0) {
				_PRODUCTS=[];
				_PRODUCTS['productID']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['productName']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['subCategory1ID']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['subCategory1']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['subCategory2ID']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['subCategory2']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['subCategory3ID']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['subCategory3']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['subCategory4ID']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['subCategory4']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['promotionProductID']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['promotionProductName']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['priceWithTax']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['priceWithoutTax']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['originalPriceWithTax']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['quantity']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['promotionQuantity']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['quantityRequested']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['quantityAdded']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['quantityAvailability']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['orderType']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['position']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['discountTypeEffective']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['default']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['default']['priceWithTax']=0;
				_PRODUCTS['default']['quantity']=0;
				_PRODUCTS['default']['length']=0;
				_PRODUCTS['giftcard']=[];
				_PRODUCTS['giftcard']['priceWithTax']=0;
				_PRODUCTS['giftcard']['quantity']=0;
				_PRODUCTS['giftcard']['length']=0;
				_PRODUCTS['uniqueProductCount']=0;
				for (i=0;i<obj.length;i++) {
					_PRODUCTS['productID'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].productID);
					_PRODUCTS['productName'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].productName);
					_PRODUCTS['productName'][i]=decodeString(_PRODUCTS['productName'][i]);
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory1ID'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].subCategory1ID,'n/a');
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory1'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].subCategory1,'n/a');
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory1'][i]=decodeString(_PRODUCTS['subCategory1'][i]);
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory2ID'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].subCategory2ID);
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory2'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].subCategory2,'n/a');
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory2'][i]=decodeString(_PRODUCTS['subCategory2'][i]);
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory3ID'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].subCategory3ID,'n/a');
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory3'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].subCategory3,'n/a');
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory3'][i]=decodeString(_PRODUCTS['subCategory3'][i]);
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory4ID'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].subCategory4ID,'n/a');
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory4'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].subCategory4,'n/a');
					_PRODUCTS['subCategory4'][i]=decodeString(_PRODUCTS['subCategory4'][i]);
					_PRODUCTS['promotionProductID'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].promotionProductID);
					_PRODUCTS['promotionProductName'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].promotionProductName);
					_PRODUCTS['promotionProductName'][i]=decodeString(_PRODUCTS['promotionProductName'][i]);
					_PRODUCTS['priceWithTax'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].priceWithTax);
					_PRODUCTS['priceWithoutTax'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].priceWithoutTax);
					_PRODUCTS['originalPriceWithTax'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].originalPriceWithTax);
					_PRODUCTS['quantity'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].quantity);
					_PRODUCTS['promotionQuantity'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].promotionQuantity);
					if (i==0) _PRODUCTS['uniqueProductCount']=1;
					else {
						if (_PRODUCTS['productID'][(i-1)]!=_PRODUCTS['productID'][i]) _PRODUCTS['uniqueProductCount']++;
					}
					_PRODUCTS['quantityRequested'][i]=_checkIfExist(String(obj[i].quantityRequested));
					_PRODUCTS['quantityAdded'][i]=_checkIfExist(String(obj[i].quantityAdded));
					_PRODUCTS['quantityAvailability'][i]=_checkIfExist(String(obj[i].quantityAvailability));
					_PRODUCTS['orderType'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].orderType);
					_PRODUCTS['position'][i]=_checkIfExist(obj[i].position);
					if (_ProductIDSGiftCards.indexOf(_PRODUCTS['productID'][i])>-1) {
						_PRODUCTS['discountTypeEffective'][i]='Gift Card';
						_PRODUCTS['giftcard']['length']++;
						_PRODUCTS['giftcard']['quantity']+=Number(_PRODUCTS['quantity'][i]);
						_PRODUCTS['giftcard']['priceWithTax']+=Number(_PRODUCTS['priceWithTax'][i])*Number(_PRODUCTS['quantity'][i]);
					}
          else {
						_PRODUCTS['discountTypeEffective'][i]='n/a'; /* enter default value if needed */
						_PRODUCTS['default']['length']++;
						_PRODUCTS['default']['quantity']+=Number(_PRODUCTS['quantity'][i]);
						_PRODUCTS['default']['priceWithTax']+=Number(_PRODUCTS['priceWithTax'][i])*Number(_PRODUCTS['quantity'][i]);
					}
					_PRODUCTS['default']['priceWithTax']=Math.round(_PRODUCTS['default']['priceWithTax']*100)/100;
					_PRODUCTS['giftcard']['priceWithTax']=Math.round(_PRODUCTS['giftcard']['priceWithTax']*100)/100;
					/* built 3rd party products */
					_PRODUCTS_3rdParty[j]=(_PRODUCTS['promotionProductID'][i]!='')?_PRODUCTS['promotionProductID'][i]:_PRODUCTS['productID'][i];
					j++;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	_DTM.productsLayerSet=1;
	_satellite.notify('A_FUNCTIONS > Object _PRODUCTS CREATED! '+_DTM.productsLayerSet);
}
/* stores value in localStorage OR cookie */
function _setValueToLocalStorageORCookie(n,v,l) {
	try {
		var _storage=window['localStorage'];
		_storage.setItem(n,v);
		_storage.removeItem(n);
		_DTM.storeIndex=1;
	}
	catch(e) {
		_satellite.notify('Error: AppM function "_setValueToLocalStorageORCookie": '+e);}
    if (_DTM.storeIndex) _storage.setItem(n,v);
    else _satellite.setCookie(n,v);
		if (l) _satellite.notify("Write to "+_DTM.storeType[_DTM.storeIndex]+" : "+n+"='"+v+"'");
}
/* reads value from localStorage OR cookie */
function _getValueFromLocalStorageORCookie(n,l) {
	var v='';
	try {v=window['localStorage'].getItem(n);_DTM.storeIndex=1;}
	catch(e){if (v==null||v=='') _satellite.readCookie(n);}
	v=v?v:'';
	if (l) _satellite.notify("Value from "+_DTM.storeType[_DTM.storeIndex]+" for "+n+"='"+v+"'");
	return v;
}
function _getProductFindingMethod() {
	/* define/get vars */
	var _intRef=_satellite.getVar('_isInternalReferrer')=='0';
	var _chnl=s._prevChannel;
	var _primCat=_satellite.getVar('PrimaryCategory');
	var _subCat1=_satellite.getVar('SubCategory_1');
	var _subCat2=_satellite.getVar('SubCategory_2');
	var _subCat3=_satellite.getVar('SubCategory_3');
	var _subCat4=_satellite.getVar('SubCategory_4');
	var _icid=_satellite.getVar('Internal Campaign');
	var _searchPhrase_woCmp=_satellite.getVar('Search - Int Searchphrase wo_cid_icid');
	var _searchPhrase_wCmp=_satellite.getVar('Search - Int Searchphrase w_cid_icid');
	var _refProdID=window._adbRefProdID;
	s.eVar60='';
	/* determine product finding method */
	if (s._pageType=='Product Detail') {
		if (s._prevRefDom!='') s.eVar60='External>Referring Domains>'+s._prevRefDom;
		if (s.products.indexOf('eVar62=')>=0) s.eVar60='Page Type>Product Detail>'+s.prop62.replace(/>/g,'|');
		if (_cartOrderType!='') s.eVar60='Page Type>Cart>'+((_cartOrderType=='reservation')?'Reservation':'Delivery')+' Cart';
		if (document.referrer=='') s.eVar60='External>Unknown>n/a';
		if (s.campaign.match(/^SEA/i)!=null) s.eVar60='External>Paid Search>'+s.campaign;
		if (s.campaign.match(/^(PSM|PSE)/i)!=null) s.eVar60='External>Price Search Engines>'+s.campaign;
		if (s.campaign.match(/^News/i)!=null) s.eVar60='External>Newsletter>'+s.campaign;
		if (s.campaign.match(/^Disp/i)!=null) s.eVar60='External>Display>'+s.campaign;
		if (s.campaign.match(/^Pros/i)!=null) s.eVar60='External>Prospects>'+s.campaign;
		if (s.campaign.match(/^Vid/i)!=null) s.eVar60='External>Video>'+s.campaign;		
		if (s.campaign.match(/^Sms/i)!=null) s.eVar60='External>SMS>'+s.campaign;		
		if (s.campaign.match(/^App/i)!=null) s.eVar60='External>Mobile App>'+s.campaign;		
    if (_chnl.match(/^Social/i)!=null) s.eVar60='External>Social>'+s._prevRefDom;
		if (_chnl.match(/^Natural Search/i)!=null) s.eVar60='External>Natural Search>'+s._prevRefDom;
	}
	if (s._pageType.match(/Product Listing/i)!=null && s._searchPhrase!='') s.eVar60='Onsite Search>Redirect>'+_searchPhrase_woCmp;
	if (s._pageType=='Searchresults') {
		if (_searchPhrase_woCmp!='') s.eVar60='Onsite Search>Searchresults>'+_searchPhrase_woCmp;
		if (_searchPhrase_wCmp!='') s.eVar60='Page Type>Searchresults>'+_searchPhrase_wCmp;
	}
	if (s._pageType.match(/Product Listing|Product Detail/i)!=null && window._autoSuggestCategory!=''&&window._autoSuggestCategory!='n/a'&&window._autoSuggestCategory.match(/product|category/i)) {
		s.eVar60='Onsite Search>Auto-Suggest-Layer>'+_searchPhrase_woCmp+'|'+window._autoSuggestCategory;
	}
	if (s._pageType.match(/Searchresults|Product Listing|Product Detail/i)!=null) {
		if (_icid.match(/^IntHan|IntPro/i)!=null) s.eVar60='Internal Campaign>Online Prospect>'+_icid;
		if (_icid.match(/^IntObj/i)!=null) s.eVar60='Internal Campaign>Object Designer>'+_icid;
		if (_icid.match(/^IntHom/i)!=null) s.eVar60='Page Type>Home>'+_icid;
	}
	if (s._pageType=='Home') s.eVar60='Page Type>Home>n/a';
	if (s.eVar60=='' && s._pageType=='Product Listing') s.eVar60='Page Type>Product Listing>'+_primCat+'|'+_subCat1+'|'+_subCat2+'|'+_subCat3+'|'+_subCat4;
	if (s._pageType=='Product Detail' && window._carouselData!='') s.eVar60='Page Type>Product Detail>'+_refProdID;
	if (s._pageType.match(/Content|no page type set/)!=null) {
		if (s._searchPhrase!='') s.eVar60='Onsite Search>Redirect>'+s._searchPhrase;
		else s.eVar60='Page Type>Content>'+_primCat+'|'+_subCat1+'|'+_subCat2+'|'+_subCat3+'|'+_subCat4;
	}
}

Si necesitas ayuda para modificar los ajustes del navegador, consulta el sitio Web del fabricante del navegador.</span>

<div class="unit size1of1 paddingClass">
<section class="mod modPageExtension">
<h1 class="webfont-bold" style="font-family: 'tazregular', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 24px;">BAUHAUS Tienda Online - Bricolaje, Jardín, Herramientas, Decoración</h1>
<p></p><p style="font-family: Arial, Verdana, sans-serif; font-size: 12px;">Tienda Online BAUHAUS: Bricolaje, Ferretería, Decoración y más. La casa más grande para tu Hogar, Taller y Jardín. Reformas, Instalaciones y Proyectos para reformar tu Baño, Cocina o cualquier estancia de tu casa.</p><p style="font-family: Arial, Verdana, sans-serif; font-size: 12px;">Prepara tu hogar con la mejor selección de jardín: casetas, muebles de exterior, toldos y carpas, iluminación exterior, maquinaria de jardín. Y para no pasar calor elige entre aires acondicionados, climatizadores, piscinas con una amplia variedad de ventiladores, climatizadores portátiles y aires acondicinados. También encontrarás accesorios de piscinas hinchables, piscinas auntomontables o piscinas infantiles. Además, complementa esta estación del año con la decoración más acorde para tu hogar: cortinas y estores, alfombras, cojines, papel pintado, vinilos decorativos, cuadros, espejos, relojes, lámparas y mucho más. Y por supuesto, sorprende a tus visitas con tu baño, decóralo con los más casi 1.000 complementos y accesorios para baños o refórmalo a tu gusto.</p><p></p>
</section>
